# Commands · C · part 6

1,480 entries, cb-refresh to change. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **cb-refresh**: Reloads the skill instructions and clears all active modes. Use mid-session when skill files have been updated and you want the new behavior to take effect without starting a new session. ([maneja81/claude-skills](https://github.com/maneja81/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/code-better/skills/code-better/commands/cb-refresh.md) · ★1)
- **cb-release**: /cb-release demo" = the milestone on staging and the owner's milestone approval; "/cb-release" = the handover and the owner's release approval, then main, the tag and the image. ([liquizzz/crewbook-build](https://github.com/liquizzz/crewbook-build/blob/HEAD/kit/commands/cb-release.md))
- **cb-remember**: Two modes: **save** (default) and **cleanup**. ([maneja81/claude-skills](https://github.com/maneja81/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/code-better/skills/code-better/commands/cb-remember.md) · ★1)
- **cb-research**: Read `commands/_shared-rules.md` if not already loaded this session. ([maneja81/claude-skills](https://github.com/maneja81/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/code-better/skills/code-better/commands/cb-research.md) · ★1)
- **cb-review**: A second opinion on a PR from Codex (Lead only): its review and a security pass, findings posted on the PR, each one accepted or rejected ([liquizzz/crewbook-build](https://github.com/liquizzz/crewbook-build/blob/HEAD/kit/commands/cb-review.md))
- **cb-review-flow**: Read `commands/_shared-rules.md` if not already loaded this session. ([maneja81/claude-skills](https://github.com/maneja81/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/code-better/skills/code-better/commands/cb-review-flow.md) · ★1)
- **cb-save-sync**: Run the 11-step save+sync protocol at session close. Wiki update + commit + handoff. User-gated at sensitive steps. ([aksheyw/context-bridge](https://github.com/aksheyw/context-bridge/blob/HEAD/skill/commands/cb-save-sync.md))
- **cb-send**: Send the prompt of your last hand-off block to the session it names, so the owner does not switch sessions ([liquizzz/crewbook-build](https://github.com/liquizzz/crewbook-build/blob/HEAD/kit/commands/cb-send.md))
- **cb-setup**: Analyze the codebase and generate all missing project setup files from scratch: `CLAUDE.md`, `MEMORY.md`, and `0-cowork/memory/`. Standalone — can be run anytime. No external skill required. ([maneja81/claude-skills](https://github.com/maneja81/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/code-better/skills/code-better/commands/cb-setup.md) · ★1)
- **cb-setup**: Set up the project in ten steps (tools, the idea with roles and autonomy, requirements, stack, look and feel, GitHub, environments, the plan approval, worktrees, sessions); run it again to go on where it stopped ([liquizzz/crewbook-build](https://github.com/liquizzz/crewbook-build/blob/HEAD/kit/commands/cb-setup.md))
- **cb-ship**: Read `commands/_shared-rules.md` if not already loaded this session. ([maneja81/claude-skills](https://github.com/maneja81/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/code-better/skills/code-better/commands/cb-ship.md) · ★1)
- **cb-spec**: Analyze a feature specification, user story, PRD, or requirements description for gaps, conflicts, ambiguities, and missing edge cases — before any planning or development starts. One-shot, read-only. ([maneja81/claude-skills](https://github.com/maneja81/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/code-better/skills/code-better/commands/cb-spec.md) · ★1)

- **cb-thorough**: Deep research mode. Trades token efficiency for completeness. Use before `cb-brainstorm` or `cb-plan` when missing a detail would produce a bad plan. ([maneja81/claude-skills](https://github.com/maneja81/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/code-better/skills/code-better/commands/cb-thorough.md) · ★1)

Safe by default — never deletes, only archives. ([maneja81/claude-skills](https://github.com/maneja81/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/code-better/skills/code-better/commands/cb-tidy.md) · ★1)

([maneja81/claude-skills](https://github.com/maneja81/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/code-better/skills/code-better/commands/cb-verify.md) · ★1)

- **ceo-loop**: Activate the CEO Decision Loop in ORBIT mode. Starts a continuous multi-iteration governance cycle: OBSERVE pending decisions → REASON with precedent → BUILD decisions → INSPECT retrograde → TURN for next iteration. The Stop hook keeps the loop alive until the queue is empty or limits are reached. Usage: /nxtg-forge:ceo-loop [max-iterations] [time-limit-minutes] ([nxtg-ai/forge-plugin](https://github.com/nxtg-ai/forge-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/nxtg-forge/commands/ceo-loop.md) · ★7)
- **ceo-loop-cancel**: Cancel the active CEO Decision Loop gracefully. Sets the loop to inactive, writes a final session summary to the progress file, and logs session statistics. Does NOT delete the decision journal — all decisions are preserved. Safe to run at any time, even if no loop is active. ([nxtg-ai/forge-plugin](https://github.com/nxtg-ai/forge-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/nxtg-forge/commands/ceo-loop-cancel.md) · ★7)
- **ceo-loop-cancel**: &gt; Gracefully stop the active CEO Decision Loop, write a final session summary with statistics, and preserve the complete decision journal for future sessions. ([nxtg-ai/forge-plugin](https://github.com/nxtg-ai/forge-plugin/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/ceo-loop-cancel.md) · ★7)
